  const solutionInsightKey=value=>norm(value)
    .normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'')
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ')
    .replace(/\s+/g,' ')
    .trim();
  const REVIEWED_SOLUTION_BY_NORMALIZED_INSIGHT=new Map(
    Object.entries(REVIEWED_SOLUTION_BY_INSIGHT).map(([key,value])=>[solutionInsightKey(key),value])
  );
  function reviewedSolutionMeta(insight){
    return REVIEWED_SOLUTION_BY_NORMALIZED_INSIGHT.get(solutionInsightKey(insight))||null;
  }
  function withSolutionMeta(record){
    const reviewed=reviewedSolutionMeta(record?.insight)||{};
    const currentDesignRef=clean(record?.designRef);
    const currentDesignHasUrl=/https?:\/\//i.test(currentDesignRef);
    return {...record,
      solutionId:clean(record?.solutionId)||reviewed.solutionId||'',
      solutionUx:clean(record?.solutionUx)||reviewed.solutionUx||'',
      designRationale:clean(record?.designRationale)||reviewed.designRationale||'',
      // Un texto de interfaz como "Abrir diseño ↗" no es una referencia.
      // Si existe una URL revisada, debe prevalecer para UI y exportaciones.
      designRef:(currentDesignHasUrl?currentDesignRef:'')||reviewed.designRef||currentDesignRef||'',
      solutionStatus:clean(record?.solutionStatus)||reviewed.solutionStatus||'',
      solutionRelation:clean(record?.solutionRelation)||reviewed.solutionRelation||''
    };
  }

    // No se fuerza una equivalencia si el origen histórico no identifica un tipo de análisis.
    'negocio':[]
  }));
  function normalizeOrigins(value){
    const raw=Array.isArray(value)?value:[value];
    const out=[];
    raw.flatMap(item=>String(item??'').split(/[;|\n]+/)).map(clean).filter(Boolean).forEach(item=>{
      const normalized=norm(item);
      let mapped=originAtomicMap.get(normalized);
      if(mapped===undefined){
        // Los nombres de consultoras, estudios o referencias concretas se consideran Desk research.
        if(/\b(mckinsey|bcg|deloitte|iata|collinson|km malta|investigación académica|investigacion academica|desk research)\b/i.test(item)){
          mapped=['Desk research'];
        }else{
          mapped=[];
        }
      }
      mapped.filter(label=>originValues.some(allowed=>norm(allowed)===norm(label))).forEach(label=>{
        if(!out.some(existing=>norm(existing)===norm(label)))out.push(label);
      });
    });
return out;
  }

    'israel':'Oriente Medio',

  function canonicalFromMap(value,map,allowed){
    const raw=clean(value);if(!raw)return '';
    const mapped=map.get(norm(raw));
    if(mapped&&(!allowed||allowed.some(item=>norm(item)===norm(mapped))))return mapped;
    if(allowed){const canonical=allowed.find(item=>norm(item)===norm(raw));if(canonical)return canonical;}
    return raw;
  }
  function normalizeRecordType(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,recordTypeAtomicMap,recordTypeValues);}
  function normalizeDevice(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,deviceAtomicMap,deviceValues);}
  function normalizeMarket(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,marketAtomicMap,marketValues);}
  function normalizeRegion(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,regionAtomicMap,regionValues);}
  function normalizeUserState(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,userStateAtomicMap);}
  function normalizeResult(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,resultAtomicMap);}
  function normalizeImplementation(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,implementationAtomicMap);}
  function normalizePlatform(value){return canonicalFromMap(value,platformAtomicMap);}

  function normalizeUxLines(value){
    const raw=Array.isArray(value)?value:[value];const out=[];
    raw.flatMap(item=>String(item??'').split(/[;|·\n]+/)).map(clean).filter(Boolean).forEach(item=>{
      const mapped=uxLineAtomicMap.get(norm(item))||[];
      mapped.filter(label=>uxDimensionValues.some(allowed=>norm(allowed)===norm(label))).forEach(label=>{
        if(!out.some(existing=>norm(existing)===norm(label)))out.push(label);
      });
    });
    return out.slice(0,2);
  }
  function deriveRegion(market){const value=normalizeMarket(market);if(!value)return '';return regionByMarket.get(norm(value))||(/multimercado|no especificado/i.test(value)?'No especificado':'');}

  function reviewedImpactUxKey(value){
    return norm(value)
      .normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'')
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ')
      .replace(/\s+/g,' ')
      .trim();
  }
  function reviewedImpactUxForInsight(insight){
    return REVIEWED_IMPACT_UX_BY_INSIGHT[reviewedImpactUxKey(insight)]||'';
  }
  function reviewedImpactUxValue(insight,current,revisionValue){
    if(revisionValue===REVIEWED_IMPACT_UX_REVISION)return clean(current);
    return reviewedImpactUxForInsight(insight)||clean(current);
  }
  globalThis.UXReviewedImpact={
    revision:REVIEWED_IMPACT_UX_REVISION,
    forInsight:reviewedImpactUxForInsight
  };

  function decorateRecord(record){
    const uxLines=normalizeUxLines(record.uxLines?.length?record.uxLines:record.uxLine);
    const analysisSource=clean(record.analysisType)
      ? record.analysisType
      : (record.origins?.length?record.origins:record.origin);
    const origins=normalizeOrigins(analysisSource);
    const market=normalizeMarket(record.market);
    const explicitRegion=normalizeRegion(record.region);
    return {...record,
      recordType:normalizeRecordType(record.recordType),
      device:normalizeDevice(record.device),
      market,
      region:explicitRegion||deriveRegion(market),
      userState:normalizeUserState(record.userState),
      status:normalizeResult(record.status),
      implementation:normalizeImplementation(record.implementation),
      platformSystem:normalizePlatform(record.platformSystem),
      impactUx:reviewedImpactUxValue(record.insight,record.impactUx,record.impactUxReviewRevision),
      origins,origin:origins.join(' · '),uxLines,uxLine:uxLines.join(' · ')
    };
  }
  const first=(obj,names)=>{
    for(const name of names){ if(obj && clean(obj[name])) return clean(obj[name]); }
    return '';
  };
  const values=(obj,names)=>{
    for(const name of names){
      if(!obj) continue;
      const v=obj[name];
      if(Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
      if(clean(v)) return [clean(v)];
    }
    return [];
  };

  function normalizedRecords(){
    if(globalThis.UXSimpleKnowledge?.allRecords){
      const structured=globalThis.UXSimpleKnowledge.allRecords(getDocs());
      if(structured.length){
        return structured.map((row,index)=>{
          const rowDoc=getDocs().find(doc=>doc.id===row.documentId);
          const sourceRow=(rowDoc?.insightRegister?.rows||[]).find(candidate=>solutionInsightKey(candidate?.insight||candidate?.['Insight / hallazgo']||'')===solutionInsightKey(row?.insight||''));
          const componentNames=splitComponentValues(row?.components||[],sourceRow?.components||[],row?.component||'',sourceRow?.component||sourceRow?.['Componente']||'');
          const initiatives=dedupe([...(Array.isArray(row.initiatives)?row.initiatives:[]),...(Array.isArray(rowDoc?.initiatives)?rowDoc.initiatives:[]),row.initiative||'',rowDoc?.initiative||'']);
          return decorateRecord({
          id:row.id||`KB-${index}`,
          docId:row.documentId||'',
          docName:row.source||row.workbook||'Fuente estructurada',
          sourceFile:row.workbook||'',
          initiative:row.initiative||rowDoc?.initiative||initiatives[0]||'',
          initiatives,
          product:row.product||'',
          funnel:row.funnel||'',
          minifunnel:row.minifunnel||'',
          screen:row.screen||'',
          component:componentNames[0]||row.component||'',
          components:componentNames,
          
          recordType:row.recordType||'',
          insight:row.insight||'',
          analysisType:(row.analysisType||((rowDoc&&typeof documentAnalysisType==='function')?documentAnalysisType(rowDoc):'')),
          origin:row.origin||'',
          device:row.channelEnvironment||row.device||'',
          market:row.market||'',
          region:row.region||'',
          userState:row.userState||'',
          evidence:row.evidence||'',
          implication:row.uxImplication||'',
          status:row.result||row.status||'',
          implementation:row.implementation||'',
          platformSystem:row.platformSystem||'',
          experiment:row.experimentPotential||'',
          recommendation:row.recommendation||'',
          openQuestion:row.openQuestion||'',
          uxLine:row.uxLine||'',
          source:row.source||'',
          notes:row.observations||'',
          impactUx:row.impactUx||'',
          confidence:row.confidence||'',
          solutionId:row.solutionId||row.solutionUxId||'',
          solutionUx:row.solutionUx||'',
          designRationale:row.designRationale||row.solutionRationale||'',
          designRef:row.designRef||row.figmaLink||'',
          solutionStatus:row.solutionStatus||'',
          solutionRelation:row.solutionRelation||'',
          ingestedAt:row.ingestedAt||rowDoc?.ingestedAt||''
          });
        });
      }
    }

    const out=[];
    for(const doc of getDocs()){
      const rows=doc?.insightRegister?.rows || [];
      rows.forEach((row,index)=>{
        out.push(decorateRecord({
          id:`${doc.id||'DOC'}-${index}`,
          docId:doc.id,
          docName:doc.name || doc.originalName || 'Documento',
          sourceFile:doc.originalName || '',
          ingestedAt:doc.ingestedAt||'',
          initiative:first(row,['Iniciativa','iniciativa','initiative']) || doc.initiative || '',
          initiatives:dedupe([...values(row,['Iniciativas','iniciativas','initiatives']),...(Array.isArray(doc.initiatives)?doc.initiatives:[]),doc.initiative||'']),
          product:first(row,['Producto','producto','product']),
          funnel:first(row,['Funnel','funnel']),
          minifunnel:first(row,['Minifunnel','minifunnel','Mini funnel','miniFunnel']),
          screen:first(row,['Pantalla','pantalla','screen']),
          component:splitComponentValues(row?.components||[],first(row,['Componente','componente','component']))[0]||'',
          components:splitComponentValues(row?.components||[],first(row,['Componente','componente','component'])),
          element:'',
          recordType:first(row,['Tipo de registro','tipo de registro','recordType','Tipo']),
          insight:first(row,['Insight / hallazgo','Insight','insight','Hallazgo','hallazgo']),
          analysisType:(typeof documentAnalysisType==='function'?documentAnalysisType(doc):''),
          origin:first(row,['Origen del hallazgo','origen del hallazgo','origin','Origen']),
          device:first(row,['Canal / entorno','canal / entorno','channelEnvironment','Dispositivo','dispositivo','device']),
          market:first(row,['Mercado','mercado','market']),
          region:first(row,['Región','Region','región','region']),
          userState:first(row,['Estado usuario','Estado del usuario','estado usuario','userState','Login','login']),
          evidence:first(row,['Evidencia / dato clave','Evidencia','evidencia','dato clave']),
          implication:first(row,['Implicación UX','implicación UX','Implicacion UX','Lectura UX','lectura UX','implication']),
          status:first(row,['Resultado','resultado','result','Estado / resultado','Estado','estado','status']),
          implementation:first(row,['Implementación','Implementacion','implementación','implementacion','implementation']),
          platformSystem:first(row,['Sistema / plataforma','sistema / plataforma','platformSystem']),
          experiment:first(row,['Potencial de experimentación','potencial de experimentación','experimentPotential']),
          recommendation:first(row,['Recomendación / oportunidad','Recomendacion / oportunidad','recomendación / oportunidad','recomendacion / oportunidad']),
          openQuestion:first(row,['Pregunta abierta','pregunta abierta']),
          uxLine:first(row,['Dimensión','Dimension','dimensión','dimension','Dimensión UX','Dimension UX','dimensión ux','dimension ux','Línea UX','Linea UX','línea ux','linea ux','Línea UX / agrupación','Linea UX / agrupacion','línea ux / agrupación','linea ux / agrupacion']),
          source:first(row,['Fuente','fuente','source']) || doc.name || '',
          notes:first(row,['Observaciones','observaciones','Comentario UX','Comentario']),
          impactUx:first(row,['Impacto UX','impacto ux','impactUx','impacto']),
          confidence:first(row,['Confianza','confianza','confidence']),
          viability:first(row,['Viabilidad','viabilidad','feasibility']),
          solutionId:first(row,['ID solución UX','ID solucion UX','id solución ux','id solucion ux','solutionId']),
          solutionUx:first(row,['Solución UX propuesta','Solucion UX propuesta','solución ux propuesta','solucion ux propuesta','solutionUx']),
          designRationale:first(row,['Fundamento de la propuesta UX','fundamento de la propuesta ux','Criterio de diseño','criterio de diseño','designRationale']),
          designRef:first(row,['Enlace Figma','enlace figma','Diseño / referencia','Diseño / referencia','designRef']),
          solutionStatus:first(row,['Estado de la solución','Estado de la solucion','estado de la solución','estado de la solucion','solutionStatus']),
          solutionRelation:first(row,['Relación con la solución','Relacion con la solucion','relación con la solución','relacion con la solucion','solutionRelation']),
          date:first(row,['Fecha','fecha','date']) || doc.exactDate || doc.documentDate || ''
        }));
      });
    }
    return out.filter(r=>r.insight);
  }

  let records=[];
  let filtered=[];
  const filters={uxLine:[]};
  const explorePageSize=10;
  let explorePage=1;
  let exploreSignature='';

  function dedupe(values){
    const seen=new Set(),out=[];
    values.map(clean).filter(Boolean).forEach(value=>{
      const key=norm(value);
      if(seen.has(key))return;
      seen.add(key);out.push(value);
    });
    return out;
  }

  function splitComponentValues(...inputs){
    const out=[];
    inputs.flat(Infinity).filter(value=>value!==undefined&&value!==null).forEach(value=>{
      String(value).split(/\s*(?:·|;|\|)\s*/).map(clean).filter(Boolean).forEach(name=>{
        if(!out.some(existing=>norm(existing)===norm(name)))out.push(name);
      });
    });
    return out;
  }

  function recordComponentNames(record){
    try{
      const governed=globalThis.UXGovernedTaxonomy?.componentNames?.(record);
      if(Array.isArray(governed)&&governed.length)return dedupe(governed);
    }catch(_){}
    return dedupe(splitComponentValues(record?.components||[],record?.component||''));
  }

  function unique(field){
    const source=field==='uxLine'
      ? records.flatMap(r=>r.uxLines||[])
      : field==='origin'
        ? records.flatMap(r=>r.origins||[])
        : field==='initiative'
          ? records.flatMap(r=>[r.initiative,...(r.initiatives||[])])
          : records.map(r=>r[field]);
    return dedupe(source).sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'es'));
  }

  function exploreTaxonomyContext(term){
    try{
      const all=(taxonomyProject?.state?.terms||[]).filter(item=>{
        const origin=String(item?.origin||'').trim();
        const governed=item?.governed===true||!/^excel revisado\s*·/i.test(origin);
        return item?.status==='canonical'&&governed;
      });
      const byId=new Map(all.map(item=>[String(item?.id||''),item]).filter(([id])=>id));
      const chain=[];const seen=new Set();let current=term;
      while(current){
        const id=String(current?.id||'');
        if(id&&seen.has(id))break;
        if(id)seen.add(id);
        chain.unshift(current);
        const parentId=String(current?.parentId||'');
        current=parentId?byId.get(parentId):null;
      }
      const context={};
      chain.forEach(item=>{
        const level=String(item?.level||'');
        const name=String(item?.canonicalName||item?.name||item?.label||'').trim();
        if(journeyLevels.has(level)&&name)context[level]=name;
      });
      return context;
    }catch(_){return {};}
  }

  function taxonomyValues(field){
    if(!journeyLevels.has(field))return [];
    try{
      if(typeof taxonomyProject==='undefined'||!taxonomyProject?.state?.terms)return [];
      let terms=taxonomyProject.state.terms.filter(term=>{
        const origin=String(term?.origin||'').trim();
        const governed=term?.governed===true||!/^excel revisado\s*·/i.test(origin);
        return term.level===field&&term.status==='canonical'&&governed;
      });
      const selectedProduct=norm(filters.product||'');
      const selectedFunnel=norm(filters.funnel||'');
      if(field==='funnel'&&selectedProduct){
        if(selectedProduct==='home')return [];
        terms=terms.filter(term=>norm(exploreTaxonomyContext(term).product||'')===selectedProduct);
      }
      if(field==='minifunnel'){
        if(!selectedFunnel)return [];
        terms=terms.filter(term=>{
          const context=exploreTaxonomyContext(term);
          if(selectedProduct&&norm(context.product||'')!==selectedProduct)return false;
          return norm(context.funnel||'')===selectedFunnel;
        });
      }
      if(field==='component'){
        const selectedMinifunnel=norm(filters.minifunnel||'');
        terms=terms.filter(term=>{
          const context=exploreTaxonomyContext(term);

          // Cada nivel respeta todo el contexto seleccionado antes.
          if(selectedProduct&&norm(context.product||'')!==selectedProduct)return false;
          if(selectedFunnel&&norm(context.funnel||'')!==selectedFunnel)return false;

          // Con minifunnel: solo componentes de ese minifunnel.
          if(selectedMinifunnel)return norm(context.minifunnel||'')===selectedMinifunnel;

          // Con funnel pero sin minifunnel: solo componentes directos del funnel.
          // Los componentes de minifunnels aparecen al seleccionar el minifunnel correspondiente.
          if(selectedFunnel)return !clean(context.minifunnel);

          // Solo con Producto: cualquier componente dentro de ese Producto.
          return true;
        });
      }
      return terms.map(term=>term.canonicalName);
    }catch(_){return [];}
  }

  function controlledTaxonomyValues(field){
    if(field==='uxLine')return controlledFilterValues.uxLine.slice();
    const labelMap={device:'Dispositivo',market:'Mercado',region:'Región',origin:'Tipo de análisis',recordType:'Tipo de registro',impactUx:'Impacto UX'};
    const label=labelMap[field];if(!label||typeof taxonomyControlledValues!=='function')return [];
    try{return taxonomyControlledValues(label);}catch(_){return [];}
  }

  function filterOptions(field){
    const fromData=unique(field);
    if(field==='funnel'&&norm(filters.product||'')==='home')return [];
    if(field==='minifunnel'&&!norm(filters.funnel||''))return [];
    if(journeyLevels.has(field)){
      // Nunca convertir valores observados en datos/Excel en vocabulario canónico.
      // Si el canon no está disponible todavía, el filtro espera al canon.
      return dedupe(taxonomyValues(field));
    }
    const governed=dedupe(controlledTaxonomyValues(field));
    if(governed.length)return governed;
    if(controlledFilterValues[field])return dedupe(controlledFilterValues[field]);
    return fromData;
  }

  function hierarchyFilterDisabled(field){
    if(field==='funnel')return norm(filters.product||'')==='home';
    if(field==='minifunnel'){
      if(!norm(filters.funnel||''))return true;
      return taxonomyValues('minifunnel').length===0;
    }
    return false;
  }

  function fillSelect(id,field,label){
    const el=document.getElementById(id); if(!el)return;
    const options=filterOptions(field).filter(value=>!(location.hostname==='ux-intelligence-consulta.onrender.com'&&field==='recordType'&&norm(value)===norm('Pregunta abierta')));
    const current=filters[field]||'';
    const emptyLabel=field==='recordType'?'Todos (sin preguntas abiertas)':'Todos';
    el.innerHTML=`<option value="">${emptyLabel}</option>`+options.map(v=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(v)}">${escapeHtml(v)}</option>`).join('');
    el.disabled=hierarchyFilterDisabled(field);
    const valid=options.some(value=>norm(value)===norm(current));
    el.value=valid?current:'';
    if(current&&!valid)filters[field]='';
  }

  function escapeHtml(v){
    return String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#039;'}[ch]));
  }

  function exploreInclusionTimestamp(value){
    const raw=clean(value);if(!raw)return NaN;
    const parsed=Date.parse(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(raw)?`${raw}T12:00:00`:raw);
    return Number.isFinite(parsed)?parsed:NaN;
  }
  function exploreInclusionMatch(value,from='',to=''){
    if(!from&&!to)return true;
    const time=exploreInclusionTimestamp(value);if(!Number.isFinite(time))return false;
    const fromTime=from?Date.parse(`${from}T00:00:00`):-Infinity;
    const toTime=to?Date.parse(`${to}T23:59:59.999`):Infinity;
    return time>=fromTime&&time<=toTime;
  }
  function exploreSortByInclusion(items,direction='newest'){
    const sign=direction==='oldest'?1:-1;
    return [...items].sort((a,b)=>{
      const ta=exploreInclusionTimestamp(a.ingestedAt),tb=exploreInclusionTimestamp(b.ingestedAt);
      const aKnown=Number.isFinite(ta),bKnown=Number.isFinite(tb);
      if(aKnown&&bKnown&&ta!==tb)return sign*(ta-tb);
      if(aKnown!==bKnown)return aKnown?-1:1;
      return 0;
    });
  }

  function apply(){
    const sort=document.getElementById('uxs-sort')?.value||'newest';
    const includedFrom=document.getElementById('uxs-included-from')?.value||'',includedTo=document.getElementById('uxs-included-to')?.value||'';
    const nextSignature=JSON.stringify({filters,sort,includedFrom,includedTo});
    if(nextSignature!==exploreSignature){explorePage=1;exploreSignature=nextSignature;}
    filtered=records.filter(r=>{
      const standardMatch=ids.every(([field])=>{
        if(!filters[field])return true;
        if(field==='initiative')return [r.initiative,...(r.initiatives||[])].some(value=>norm(value)===norm(filters[field]));
        if(field==='origin')return (r.origins||[]).some(value=>norm(value)===norm(filters[field]));
        if(field==='component')return recordComponentNames(r).some(value=>norm(value)===norm(filters[field]));
        return norm(r[field])===norm(filters[field]);
      });
      const selectedLines=Array.isArray(filters.uxLine)?filters.uxLine:[];
      const lineMatch=!selectedLines.length||selectedLines.some(selected=>(r.uxLines||[]).some(line=>norm(line)===norm(selected)));
      const defaultTypeMatch=norm(r.recordType)!==norm('Pregunta abierta');
      const designMode=filters.designAssociated||'';
      const hasDesign=!!(String(r.solutionId||'').trim()||String(r.designRef||'').trim());
      const designMatch=!designMode||(designMode==='with'?hasDesign:!hasDesign);
      return standardMatch&&defaultTypeMatch&&lineMatch&&designMatch&&exploreInclusionMatch(r.ingestedAt,includedFrom,includedTo);
    });
    filtered=exploreSortByInclusion(filtered,sort);
    render();
  }


  const duplicateStopWords=new Set([
    'para','como','desde','hasta','entre','sobre','donde','cuando','porque','aunque','tambien','también','solo','sólo','esta','este','estos','estas',
    'usuario','usuarios','usuaria','usuarias','identificado','identificada','identificados','identificadas','logado','logada','logados','logadas',
    'desktop','mobile','booking','suma','experiencia','presenta','presentan','muestra','muestran','mayor','menor','forma','parte','nivel','niveles',
    'relativo','relativa','claramente','observada','observado','comportamiento','compra','valor','datos','sesiones','analizadas','analizados',
    'puede','pueden','debe','deben','tiene','tienen','hacia','antes','despues','después','dentro','fuera','mucho','mucha','mismo','misma'
  ]);

  function duplicateTokens(value){
    return [...new Set(
      norm(value)
        .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s]/gu,' ')
        .split(/\s+/)
        .filter(word=>word.length>3&&!duplicateStopWords.has(word))
    )];
  }

  function duplicateContextValue(value){
    const key=norm(value);
    const aliases=new Map([
      ['disponibilidad','availability'],
      ['availability/dispo','availability'],
      ['dispo','availability'],
      ['pasajeros','passengers'],
      ['passengers/paxes','passengers'],
      ['paxes','passengers'],
      ['pago','payment'],
      ['resumen','shopping cart'],
      ['summary','shopping cart'],
      ['resumen / shopping cart','shopping cart'],
      ['summary / shopping cart','shopping cart']
    ]);
    return aliases.get(key)||key;
  }

  function duplicateSpecific(value){
    const v=duplicateContextValue(value);
    return !['','todos','todas','transversal','multipantalla','booking transversal','vision global','visión global','no especificado','no especificada'].includes(v);
  }

  function duplicateTypeGroup(value){
    const v=norm(value);
    if(!v)return '';
    if(v.includes('insight')||v.includes('hallazgo'))return 'insight';
    if(v.includes('evidencia interna'))return 'evidencia interna';
    if(v.includes('evidencia externa'))return 'evidencia externa';
    if(v.includes('pregunta'))return 'pregunta';
    if(v.includes('hipotes'))return 'hipotesis';
    if(v.includes('inferencia'))return 'inferencia';
    if(v.includes('oportunidad'))return 'oportunidad';
    if(v.includes('restric'))return 'restriccion';
    if(v.includes('decision'))return 'decision';
    return v;
  }

  function duplicateContextCompatible(a,b){
    const aType=duplicateTypeGroup(a.recordType),bType=duplicateTypeGroup(b.recordType);
    if(aType&&bType&&aType!==bType)return false;

    for(const field of ['product','funnel','minifunnel','component']){
      const av=duplicateContextValue(a[field]),bv=duplicateContextValue(b[field]);
      if(duplicateSpecific(av)&&duplicateSpecific(bv)&&av!==bv)return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  function duplicateSimilarity(a,b){
    const aTitle=norm(a.insight).replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s]/gu,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    const bTitle=norm(b.insight).replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s]/gu,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    if(!aTitle||!bTitle)return 0;
    if(aTitle===bTitle)return 1;

    const aTokens=duplicateTokens(a.insight),bTokens=duplicateTokens(b.insight);
    if(aTokens.length<3||bTokens.length<3)return 0;

    const aSet=new Set(aTokens),bSet=new Set(bTokens);
    const overlap=[...aSet].filter(token=>bSet.has(token)).length;
    const union=new Set([...aSet,...bSet]).size;
    const jaccard=union?overlap/union:0;
    const containment=overlap/Math.min(aSet.size,bSet.size);

    if(overlap>=4&&jaccard>=.68&&containment>=.80)return Math.max(jaccard,containment);
    if(overlap>=5&&jaccard>=.55&&containment>=.75)return Math.max(jaccard,containment);
    return 0;
  }


  function duplicateRecordReviewKey(record){
    const parts=[
      clean(record?.docId||record?.documentId||''),
      clean(record?.sourceFile||record?.docName||record?.source||''),
      clean(record?.id||''),
      norm(record?.insight||'')
    ];
    return parts.join('¦');
  }

  function duplicatePairKey(a,b){
    return [duplicateRecordReviewKey(a),duplicateRecordReviewKey(b)].sort().join(' ↔ ');
  }

  function duplicateReviewRead(){
    try{
      const value=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(DUPLICATE_REVIEW_STORAGE_KEY)||'{}');
      return value&&typeof value==='object'?value:{};
    }catch(_){return {};}
  }

  function duplicateAutoDecision(a,b){
    const aTitle=norm(a?.insight||'').replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s]/gu,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    const bTitle=norm(b?.insight||'').replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s]/gu,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();

    // Criterio validado sobre la base actual:
    // mismo conocimiento formulado exactamente igual + contexto compatible = duplicado.
    // Las afinidades temáticas o frases parecidas NO se confirman automáticamente.
    if(aTitle&&aTitle===bTitle&&duplicateContextCompatible(a,b))return 'confirmed';

    return '';
  }

  function duplicateReviewDecision(a,b){
    const manual=duplicateReviewRead()[duplicatePairKey(a,b)]?.decision||'';
    return manual||duplicateAutoDecision(a,b);
  }

  function duplicateReviewWrite(a,b,decision){
    const state=duplicateReviewRead();
    const key=duplicatePairKey(a,b);
    if(!decision){
      delete state[key];
    }else{
      state[key]={
        decision,
        at:new Date().toISOString(),
        a:{id:a.id||'',insight:a.insight||'',source:a.sourceFile||a.docName||a.source||''},
        b:{id:b.id||'',insight:b.insight||'',source:b.sourceFile||b.docName||b.source||''}
      };
    }
    try{localStorage.setItem(DUPLICATE_REVIEW_STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(state));}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof safeStorageWrite==='function')safeStorageWrite({project:true});}catch(_){}
  }

  function duplicateCandidatePool(record){
    return records
      .filter(candidate=>{
        if(candidate.id===record.id)return false;
        if(!duplicateContextCompatible(record,candidate))return false;
        return duplicateSimilarity(record,candidate)>0;
      })
      .map(candidate=>({
        record:candidate,
        score:duplicateSimilarity(record,candidate),
        decision:duplicateReviewDecision(record,candidate)
      }))
      .sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score)
      .slice(0,5);
  }

  function duplicateMatches(record){
    return duplicateCandidatePool(record).filter(match=>match.decision!=='not-duplicate');
  }

  function duplicateCount(record){
    return duplicateMatches(record).length;
  }

  function duplicateListBadge(record){
    const matches=duplicateMatches(record);
    if(!matches.length)return '';
    const confirmed=matches.filter(match=>match.decision==='confirmed').length;
    if(confirmed){
      return `<span class="uxs-duplicate is-confirmed">${confirmed===1?'Duplicado confirmado':`${confirmed} duplicados confirmados`}</span>`;
    }
    const count=matches.length;
    return `<span class="uxs-duplicate">${count} posible${count===1?' duplicado':'s duplicados'}</span>`;
  }

  function duplicateHeaderBadge(record){
    const matches=duplicateMatches(record);
    if(!matches.length)return '';
    const confirmed=matches.some(match=>match.decision==='confirmed');
    return confirmed
      ? '<span class="uxs-duplicate is-confirmed">Duplicado confirmado</span>'
      : '<span class="uxs-duplicate">Posible duplicado</span>';
  }

  function duplicateReviewStatus(match){
    if(match.decision==='confirmed')return '<span class="status-badge is-success">Duplicado confirmado</span>';
    if(match.decision==='not-duplicate')return '<span class="status-badge">No es duplicado</span>';
    return '<span class="uxs-duplicate">Pendiente de revisar</span>';
  }

  function duplicateReviewMarkup(record){
    const candidates=duplicateCandidatePool(record);
    if(!candidates.length)return '';
    return `<section class="drawer-section">
      <h3>Revisión de duplicados</h3>
      <p>UX Intel detecta duplicados exactos solo dentro de contextos compatibles. Las similitudes temáticas quedan como señales para revisión y nunca fusionan ni eliminan registros automáticamente.</p>
      <div class="relation-list">${candidates.map(match=>{
        const item=match.record;
        const path=compactContextPath(item);
        const source=item.sourceFile||item.docName||item.source||'';
        const meta=[item.recordType||'',path.length?`Dónde aplica: ${path.join(' › ')}`:'',source?`Fuente: ${source}`:''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
        const confirmDisabled=match.decision==='confirmed'?' disabled aria-pressed="true"':'';
        const rejectDisabled=match.decision==='not-duplicate'?' disabled aria-pressed="true"':'';
        return `<div class="relation-row duplicate-review-row">
          <div class="relation-copy">
            <strong>${escapeHtml(item.insight)}</strong>
            <span>${escapeHtml(meta)}</span>
            <div class="duplicate-review-status">${duplicateReviewStatus(match)}</div>
            <div class="duplicate-review-actions">
              <button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-duplicate-open="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">Ver registro</button>
              <button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-duplicate-decision="confirmed" data-record-id="${escapeHtml(record.id)}" data-candidate-id="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"${confirmDisabled}>Confirmar duplicado</button>
              <button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-duplicate-decision="not-duplicate" data-record-id="${escapeHtml(record.id)}" data-candidate-id="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"${rejectDisabled}>No es duplicado</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>`;
      }).join('')}</div>
    </section>`;
  }

  function isTransversalRecord(record){
    const explicit=[record?.funnel,record?.screen,record?.component,record?.element].map(norm);
    return explicit.some(value=>['transversal','todas las pantallas','todas'].includes(value));
  }

  function recordTypeClass(value){
    const text=norm(value);
    if(text.includes('evidencia interna'))return 'is-evidence-internal';
    if(text.includes('evidencia externa'))return 'is-evidence-external';
    if(text.includes('hipotes')||text.includes('inferencia')||text.includes('restric')||text.includes('pregunta'))return 'is-attention';
    if(text.includes('oportunidad'))return 'is-opportunity';
    if(text.includes('decision')||text.includes('positivo')||text.includes('valid'))return 'is-success';
    if(text.includes('insight'))return 'is-insight';
    return 'is-evidence';
  }

  function compactContextPath(record){
    const componentLabel=recordComponentNames(record).join(' · ');
    const values=[record.product,record.funnel,record.minifunnel,componentLabel].map(clean).filter(Boolean);
    const out=[];
    values.forEach(value=>{if(!out.length||norm(out[out.length-1])!==norm(value))out.push(value);});
    return out;
  }

  function render(){
    const count=document.getElementById('uxs-result-count');
    if(count)count.textContent=`${filtered.length} ${filtered.length===1?'resultado':'resultados'}`;

    const chips=document.getElementById('uxs-active-filters');
    const active=ids.filter(([field])=>filters[field]);
    const activeLines=Array.isArray(filters.uxLine)?filters.uxLine:[];
    const activeDesign=filters.designAssociated?{field:'designAssociated',label:filters.designAssociated==='with'?'Con diseño asociado':'Sin diseño asociado'}:null;
    const dateValues=[['uxs-included-from','Incorporado desde'],['uxs-included-to','Incorporado hasta']].map(([id,label])=>({id,label,value:document.getElementById(id)?.value||''})).filter(item=>item.value);
    const activeCount=active.length+activeLines.length+dateValues.length+(activeDesign?1:0);
    if(chips){
      chips.hidden=!activeCount;
      chips.innerHTML=activeCount
        ? `<span class="active-filter-label">Filtros activos</span>`+
          active.map(([field,id])=>`<button class="uxs-filter-chip" type="button" data-uxs-remove="${escapeHtml(field)}">${escapeHtml(filters[field])}<span aria-hidden="true">×</span></button>`).join('')+
          (activeDesign?`<button class="uxs-filter-chip" type="button" data-uxs-remove="designAssociated">${escapeHtml(activeDesign.label)}<span aria-hidden="true">×</span></button>`:'')+
          activeLines.map(line=>`<button class="uxs-filter-chip" type="button" data-uxs-remove-line="${escapeHtml(line)}">${escapeHtml(line)}<span aria-hidden="true">×</span></button>`).join('')+
          dateValues.map(item=>`<button class="uxs-filter-chip" type="button" data-uxs-remove-date="${item.id}">${escapeHtml(item.label)}: ${escapeHtml(item.value.split('-').reverse().join('/'))}<span aria-hidden="true">×</span></button>`).join('')+
          `<button class="clear-filter-button" type="button" data-uxs-clear>Limpiar filtros</button>`
        : '';
    }

    const list=document.getElementById('uxs-results-list');
    if(!list)return;
    const pagination=document.getElementById('uxs-pagination');
    if(!filtered.length){list.innerHTML='<div class="uxs-empty">No hay registros que cumplan los filtros aplicados.</div>';if(pagination)pagination.innerHTML='';return;}

    const totalPages=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(filtered.length/explorePageSize));
    explorePage=Math.min(Math.max(1,explorePage),totalPages);
    const pageRecords=filtered.slice((explorePage-1)*explorePageSize,explorePage*explorePageSize);

    list.innerHTML=pageRecords.map(r=>{
      const dup=duplicateCount(r);
      const transversal=isTransversalRecord(r);
      return `<article class="uxs-result${transversal?' is-transversal':''}" data-uxs-id="${escapeHtml(r.id)}" tabindex="0">
        <div class="uxs-result-main">
          <div class="uxs-result-labels">
            <span class="uxs-result-type ${recordTypeClass(r.recordType)}">${escapeHtml(r.recordType||'Insight')}</span>
            ${transversal?'<span class="uxs-scope-tag">Transversal</span>':''}
          </div>
          ${compactContextPath(r).length?`<p class="uxs-result-context-path">${compactContextPath(r).map(escapeHtml).join(' › ')}</p>`:''}
          <h3>${escapeHtml(r.insight)}</h3>
          ${r.implication?`<p class="uxs-result-evidence">${escapeHtml(r.implication)}</p>`:''}
          ${(r.impactUx||r.uxLine)?`<div class="uxs-result-meta-row">
            ${r.impactUx?`<span class="uxs-result-impact ${impactClass(r.impactUx)}"><strong>Impacto UX</strong><em>${escapeHtml(r.impactUx)}</em></span>`:''}
            ${r.uxLine?`<span class="uxs-result-line-meta"><strong>Dimensión UX</strong><span>${escapeHtml(r.uxLine)}</span></span>`:''}
          </div>`:''}
        </div>
        <div class="uxs-result-side">
          ${r.solutionId||r.designRef?`<span class="uxs-solution-badge">Diseño relacionado</span>`:''}
          ${duplicateListBadge(r)}
          <button class="view-link" type="button">Ver detalle</button>
        </div>
      </article>`;
    }).join('');
    if(pagination)pagination.innerHTML=paginationMarkup({page:explorePage,total:filtered.length,size:explorePageSize,label:'resultados',target:'explore',controls:'uxs-results-list'});
  }

  function solutionSection(record){
    if(!record?.solutionId&&!record?.solutionUx&&!record?.designRef)return '';
    const status=clean(record.solutionStatus);
    const relation=clean(record.solutionRelation);
    const figma=clean(record.designRef);
    return `<section class="drawer-section drawer-section-emphasis ux-solution-related">
      <div class="ux-solution-topline">
        <h3>Solución UX relacionada</h3>
        ${record.solutionId?`<span class="ux-solution-id">${escapeHtml(record.solutionId)}</span>`:''}
      </div>
      ${record.solutionUx?`<p class="ux-solution-title">${escapeHtml(record.solutionUx)}</p>`:''}
      ${(relation||status)?`<dl class="ux-solution-meta">
        ${relation?`<div><dt>Relación de este hallazgo</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(relation)}</dd></div>`:''}
        ${status?`<div><dt>Estado</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(status)}</dd></div>`:''}
      </dl>`:''}
      ${record.designRationale?`<div class="ux-solution-rationale"><h4>Por qué se plantea</h4><p>${escapeHtml(record.designRationale)}</p></div>`:''}
      ${figma?`<a class="action-button ux-figma-link" href="${escapeHtml(figma)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Ver diseño en Figma <span aria-hidden="true">↗</span></a>`:''}
    </section>`;
  }

  function openDetail(id){
    const r=records.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!r)return;
    const duplicates=duplicateMatches(r);
    const dup=duplicates.length;
    const path=compactContextPath(r);
    const transversal=isTransversalRecord(r);
    const initiativeValues=dedupe([r.initiative,...(r.initiatives||[])]);
    const meta=[
      initiativeValues.length?`<div><span>Iniciativa</span><strong>${initiativeValues.map(escapeHtml).join(' · ')}</strong></div>`:'',
      path.length?`<div><span>Dónde aplica</span><strong>${path.map(escapeHtml).join(' › ')}</strong></div>`:'',
      clean(r.device)?`<div><span>Dispositivo</span><strong>${escapeHtml(r.device)}</strong></div>`:'',
      clean(r.market)?`<div><span>Mercado</span><strong>${escapeHtml(r.market)}</strong></div>`:'',
      clean(r.region)?`<div><span>Región</span><strong>${escapeHtml(r.region)}</strong></div>`:'',
      clean(r.origin)?`<div><span>Tipo de análisis</span><strong>${escapeHtml(r.origin)}</strong></div>`:'',
      clean(r.impactUx)?`<div><span>Impacto UX</span><strong><span class="status-badge ${impactClass(r.impactUx)}">${escapeHtml(r.impactUx)}</span></strong></div>`:''
    ].filter(Boolean).join('');
    const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker"><span class="uxs-result-type ${recordTypeClass(r.recordType)}">${escapeHtml(r.recordType||'Insight')}</span>${transversal?'<span class="uxs-scope-tag">Transversal</span>':''}${duplicateHeaderBadge(r)}</div><h2>${escapeHtml(r.insight)}</h2>${meta?`<div class="insight-drawer-meta">${meta}</div>`:''}`;
    const sourceFile=r.sourceFile||r.docName||r.source||'';
    const body=`${r.evidence?`<section class="drawer-section drawer-section-primary"><h3>Evidencia clave</h3><p>${escapeHtml(r.evidence)}</p></section>`:''}
      ${r.implication?`<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Lectura UX</h3><p>${escapeHtml(r.implication)}</p></section>`:''}
      ${r.recommendation?`<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Recomendación / oportunidad</h3><p>${escapeHtml(r.recommendation)}</p></section>`:''}
      ${r.openQuestion?`<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Pregunta abierta</h3><p>${escapeHtml(r.openQuestion)}</p></section>`:''}
      ${r.uxLine?`<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Dimensión UX</h3><p>${escapeHtml(r.uxLine)}</p></section>`:''}
      ${r.notes?`<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Observaciones</h3><p>${escapeHtml(r.notes)}</p></section>`:''}
      ${solutionSection(r)}
      ${sourceFile?`<section class="drawer-section"><h3>Archivo fuente</h3><p>${escapeHtml(sourceFile)}</p></section>`:''}
      ${duplicateReviewMarkup(r)}
      <div class="drawer-footer"><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-close-editor>Cerrar</button></div>`;
    if(typeof openDrawer==='function') openDrawer(header,body);
  }

  globalThis.__uxOpenExploreRecord=openDetail;

  if(!globalThis.__uxDuplicateReviewBound){
    globalThis.__uxDuplicateReviewBound=true;
    document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
      const openButton=event.target.closest('[data-duplicate-open]');
      if(openButton){
        event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();
        const candidateId=openButton.dataset.duplicateOpen;
        if(candidateId)openDetail(candidateId);
        return;
      }

      const decisionButton=event.target.closest('[data-duplicate-decision]');
      if(!decisionButton)return;
      event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();
      const record=records.find(item=>String(item.id)===String(decisionButton.dataset.recordId));
      const candidate=records.find(item=>String(item.id)===String(decisionButton.dataset.candidateId));
      if(!record||!candidate)return;
      const decision=decisionButton.dataset.duplicateDecision;
      duplicateReviewWrite(record,candidate,decision);
      render();
      openDetail(record.id);
      try{
        if(typeof showToast==='function'){
          showToast(
            decision==='confirmed'?'Duplicado confirmado':'Marcado como no duplicado',
            decision==='confirmed'
              ?'La relación queda registrada, pero los registros siguen separados hasta que decidas consolidarlos.'
              :'UX Intel dejará de mostrar esta pareja como posible duplicado.'
          );
        }
      }catch(_){}
    });
  }

  function downloadFilteredCsv(cols,filename,valueTransform,rowsToExport=filtered){
    const exportRows=Array.isArray(rowsToExport)?rowsToExport:filtered;
    if(!exportRows.length){alert('No hay resultados para exportar.');return;}
    const q=v=>'"'+String(v??'').replace(/"/g,'""')+'"';
    const transform=typeof valueTransform==='function'?valueTransform:((value)=>value);
    const csv=[
      cols.map(c=>q(c[0])).join(';'),
      ...exportRows.map(r=>cols.map(c=>q(transform(r[c[1]],c[1],r))).join(';'))
    ].join('\n');
    const blob=new Blob(["\ufeff"+csv],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8;'});
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=filename;a.click();
    setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
  }
